## P9 — remaining regenerated artifacts

- Inexact Newton: Experimental one-dimensional full backward-Euler nonlinear-solve measurement. All methods solve the same nonlinear equation to the same residual tolerance. ⟦`pme_dst_tau_blend_inexact_newton.json#/scope`⟧
- Validation: Experimental one-dimensional scaling, solve-time, and evolved-state validation; it is not a public solver component. ⟦`pme_dst_tau_blend_validation.json#/scope`⟧
- Stage-1 validation: Experimental one-dimensional preconditioned linear-solve validation. It does not establish full nonlinear time-integration work precision. ⟦`pme_dst_tau_blend_stage1_validation.json#/scope`⟧
- Proof of concept: Experimental input-weighted DST/tau blend for first Newton systems at Barenblatt initial states; it is not a public solver component. ⟦`pme_dst_tau_blend_poc.json#/scope`⟧
